      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.09">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.09 FILING REQUIREMENTS FOR TAXES WITHHELD FROM WAGES, FROM COMPENSATION OF ENTERTAINERS, AND FROM PAYMENTS TO OUT-OF-STATE CONTRACTORS; AND TAXES WITHHELD BY PARTNERSHIPS AND SMALL BUSINESS CORPORATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.09.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Returns.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employer who is required to deduct and withhold tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.2a">290.92, subdivision 2a</a> or 3, and a person required to deduct and withhold tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>, must file a return with the commissioner for each quarterly period unless otherwise prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) A person or corporation required to make deposits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.9201#stat.290.9201.8">290.9201, subdivision 8</a>, must file an entertainer withholding tax return with the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) A person required to withhold an amount under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.9705#stat.290.9705.1">290.9705, subdivision 1</a>, must file a return.</p>
          <p>(d) A partnership required to deduct and withhold tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.4b">290.92, subdivision 4b</a>, must file a return.</p>
          <p>(e) An S corporation required to deduct and withhold tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.4c">290.92, subdivision 4c</a>, must also file a return.</p>
          <p>(f) The commissioner shall prescribe the content, format, and manner of the returns pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.30">270C.30</a>. Every return for taxes withheld must be signed by the employer, entertainment entity, contract payor, partnership, or S corporation, or a designee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.09.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Withholding statement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A person required to deduct and withhold from an employee a tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.2a">290.92, subdivision 2a</a> or 3, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>, or who would have been required to deduct and withhold a tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.2a">290.92, subdivision 2a</a> or 3, or persons required to withhold tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>, determined without regard to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.19">290.92, subdivision 19</a>, if the employee or payee had claimed no more than one withholding allowance, or who paid wages or made payments not subject to withholding under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.2a">290.92, subdivision 2a</a> or 3, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>, to an employee or person receiving royalty payments in excess of $600, or who has entered into a voluntary withholding agreement with a payee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.20">290.92, subdivision 20</a>, must give every employee or person receiving royalty payments in respect to the remuneration paid by the person to the employee or person receiving royalty payments during the calendar year, on or before January 31 of the succeeding year, or, if employment is terminated before the close of the calendar year, within 30 days after the date of receipt of a written request from the employee if the 30-day period ends before January 31, a written statement showing the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) name of the person;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the name of the employee or payee and the employee's or payee's Social Security account number;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the total amount of wages as that term is defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.1">290.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (1); the total amount of remuneration subject to withholding under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.20">290.92, subdivision 20</a>; the amount of sick pay as required under section 6051(f) of the Internal Revenue Code; and the amount of royalties subject to withholding under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the total amount deducted and withheld as tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.2a">290.92, subdivision 2a </a>or 3, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.923#stat.290.923.2">290.923, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The statement required to be furnished by paragraph (a) with respect to any remuneration must be furnished at those times, must contain the information required, and must be in the form the commissioner prescribes.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner may prescribe rules providing for reasonable extensions of time, not in excess of 30 days, to employers or payers required to give the statements to their employees or payees under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A duplicate of any statement made under this subdivision and in accordance with rules prescribed by the commissioner must be filed with the commissioner on or before January 31 of the year after the payments were made.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) If an employer cancels the employer's Minnesota withholding account number required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.24">290.92, subdivision 24</a>, the information required by paragraph (d), must be filed with the commissioner within 30 days of the end of the quarter in which the employer cancels its account number.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The employer must submit the statements required to be sent to the commissioner. The commissioner shall prescribe the content, format, and manner of the statement pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.30">270C.30</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) A "third-party bulk filer" as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92#stat.290.92.30">290.92, subdivision 30</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), must submit the returns required by this subdivision and subdivision 1, paragraph (a), with the commissioner by electronic means.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.09.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Federal annuities; tax withholding request.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue shall participate with the United States Office of Personnel Management in a program of voluntary state income tax withholding on the federal annuities of retired federal employees. Upon the request of the taxpayer to the commissioner of revenue, and only on request of the taxpayer, the commissioner shall provide for state income tax withholding on federal annuities paid to the taxpayer.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.09.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.4.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.6.46.0">1991 c 291 art 6 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.6.19.0">1992 c 511 art 6 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.2.6.0">1993 c 375 art 2 s 6</a>; art 8 s 1,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.1.24.0">1994 c 587 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/31/#laws.1.4.0">1997 c 31 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/84/#laws.6.19.0">1997 c 84 art 6 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/300/#laws.1.1.0">1998 c 300 art 1 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.11.3.0">2008 c 154 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/389/#laws.3.2.0">2010 c 389 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.13.3.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 13 s 3</a>; art 16 s 24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/14/#laws.12.2.0">1Sp2021 c 14 art 12 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.10">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.10 FILING REQUIREMENTS FOR ESTATE TAX RETURNS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.10.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Return required.</span></h2>
          <p>In the case of a decedent who has an interest in property with a situs in Minnesota, the personal representative must submit a Minnesota estate tax return to the commissioner, on a form prescribed by the commissioner, if:</p>
          <p>(1) a federal estate tax return is required to be filed; or</p>
          <p>(2) the sum of the federal gross estate and federal adjusted taxable gifts, as defined in section 2001(b) of the Internal Revenue Code, made within three years of the date of the decedent's death exceeds $1,200,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2014; $1,400,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2015; $1,600,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2016; $2,100,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2017; $2,400,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2018; $2,700,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2019; and $3,000,000 for estates of decedents dying in 2020 and thereafter.</p>
          <p>The return must contain a computation of the Minnesota estate tax due. The return must be signed by the personal representative.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.10.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Recapture tax return required.</span></h2>
          <p>If a disposition or cessation as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.03#stat.291.03.11">291.03, subdivision 11</a>, paragraph (a), has occurred, the qualified heir, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.03#stat.291.03.8">291.03, subdivision 8</a>, paragraph (c), or personal representative of the decedent's estate must submit a recapture tax return to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.10.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Documents required.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may designate on the return the documents that are required to be filed together with the return to determine the computation of tax.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.10.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, the definitions contained in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.005">291.005</a> apply.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.10.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.5.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/31/#laws.1.5.0">1997 c 31 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.12.10.0">2002 c 377 art 12 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.3.1.0">2003 c 127 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/334/#laws.0.1.0">2010 c 334 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.7.3.0">2013 c 143 art 7 s 3</a>; art 15 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/150/#laws.3.1.0">2014 c 150 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.1.2.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 1 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.11">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.11 FILING REQUIREMENTS FOR SALES AND USE TAX RETURNS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.11.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Return required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.18#stat.289A.18.4">289A.18, subdivision 4</a>, for the month in which taxes imposed by chapter 297A are payable, or for which a return is due, a return for the preceding reporting period must be filed with the commissioner. The commissioner shall prescribe the content, format, and manner of the returns pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.30">270C.30</a>. A person making sales at retail at two or more places of business may file a consolidated return subject to rules prescribed by the commissioner. In computing the dollar amount of items on the return, the amounts are rounded off to the nearest whole dollar, disregarding amounts less than 50 cents and increasing amounts of 50 cents to 99 cents to the next highest dollar.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding this subdivision, a person who is not required to hold a sales tax permit under chapter 297A and who makes annual purchases, for use in a trade or business, of less than $18,500, or a person who is not required to hold a sales tax permit and who makes purchases for personal use, that are subject to the use tax imposed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.63">297A.63</a>, may file an annual use tax return. The commissioner shall prescribe the content, format, and manner of the return pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.30">270C.30</a>. If a person who qualifies for an annual use tax reporting period is required to obtain a sales tax permit or makes use tax purchases, for use in a trade or business, in excess of $18,500 during the calendar year, the reporting period must be considered ended at the end of the month in which the permit is applied for or the purchase in excess of $18,500 is made and a return must be filed for the preceding reporting period.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) and (b), a person prohibited by the person's religious beliefs from using electronics shall be allowed to file by mail, without any additional fees. The filer must notify the commissioner of revenue of the intent to file by mail on a form prescribed by the commissioner. A return filed under this paragraph must be postmarked no later than the day the return is due in order to be considered filed on a timely basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.11.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/277/#laws.1.98.0">2008 c 277 art 1 s 98</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.11.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Who must file return.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of the sales tax, a return must be filed by a retailer who is required to hold a permit. For the purposes of the use tax, a return must be filed by a retailer required to collect the tax and by a person buying any items, the storage, use or other consumption of which is subject to the use tax, who has not paid the use tax to a retailer required to collect the tax. The returns must be signed by the person filing the return or by the person's agent duly authorized in writing.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.11.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.6.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/249/#laws.0.31.0">1991 c 249 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.8.3.0">1991 c 291 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.8.2.0">1992 c 511 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.2.7.0">1993 c 375 art 2 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.2.1.0">1994 c 587 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/31/#laws.2.4.0">1997 c 31 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.44.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.5.2.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.4.2.0">2009 c 88 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.16.25.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 16 s 25</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.12">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.12 FILING REQUIREMENTS FOR INFORMATION RETURNS AND REPORTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.7.26.0">1992 c 511 art 7 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Returns required of banks; common trust funds.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require a bank maintaining a common trust fund to file with the commissioner a return for a taxable year, stating specifically with respect to the fund, the items of gross income and deductions provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.281#stat.290.281.1">290.281, subdivision 1</a>. The return must include the names and addresses of the participants entitled to share the net income if distributed and the amount of the proportionate share of each participant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Returns or reports by partnerships, fiduciaries, and S corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Partnerships must file a return with the commissioner for each taxable year. The return must conform to the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.311">290.311</a>, and must include the names and addresses of the partners entitled to a distributive share in their taxable net income, gain, loss, or credit, and the amount of the distributive share to which each is entitled. A partnership required to file a return for a partnership taxable year must furnish a copy of the information required to be shown on the return to a person who is a partner at any time during the taxable year, on or before the day on which the return for the taxable year was filed.</p>
          <p>(b) The fiduciary of an estate or trust making the return required to be filed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.08#stat.289A.08.2">289A.08, subdivision 2</a>, for a taxable year must give a beneficiary who receives a distribution from the estate or trust with respect to the taxable year or to whom any item with respect to the taxable year is allocated, a statement containing the information required to be shown on the return, on or before the date on which the return was filed.</p>
          <p>(c) An S corporation must file a return with the commissioner for a taxable year during which an election under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.9725">290.9725</a> is in effect, stating specifically the names and addresses of the persons owning stock in the corporation at any time during the taxable year, the number of shares of stock owned by a shareholder at all times during the taxable year, the shareholder's pro rata share of each item of the corporation for the taxable year, and other information the commissioner requires. An S corporation required to file a return under this paragraph for any taxable year must furnish a copy of the information shown on the return to the person who is a shareholder at any time during the taxable year, on or before the day on which the return for the taxable year was filed.</p>
          <p>(d) The partnership or S corporation return must be signed by someone designated by the partnership or S corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Returns by persons, corporations, cooperatives, governmental entities, or school districts.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner may by notice and demand require to the extent required by section 6041 of the Internal Revenue Code, a person, corporation, or cooperative, the state of Minnesota and its political subdivisions, and a city, county, and school district in Minnesota, making payments in the regular course of a trade or business during the taxable year to any person or corporation of $600 or more on account of rents or royalties, or of $10 or more on account of interest, or $10 or more on account of dividends or patronage dividends, or $600 or more on account of either wages, salaries, commissions, fees, prizes, awards, pensions, annuities, or any other fixed or determinable gains, profits or income, not otherwise reportable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.09#stat.289A.09.2">289A.09, subdivision 2</a>, or on account of earnings of $10 or more distributed to its members by savings associations or credit unions chartered under the laws of this state or the United States, (1) to file with the commissioner a return (except in cases where a valid agreement to participate in the combined federal and state information reporting system has been entered into, and the return is filed only with the commissioner of internal revenue under the applicable filing and informational reporting requirements of the Internal Revenue Code) with respect to the payments in excess of the amounts named, giving the names and addresses of the persons to whom the payments were made, the amounts paid to each, and (2) to make a return with respect to the total number of payments and total amount of payments, for each category of income named, which were in excess of the amounts named. This subdivision does not apply to the payment of interest or dividends to a person who was a nonresident of Minnesota for the entire year.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For payments for which a return is covered by paragraph (a), regardless of whether the commissioner has required filing under paragraph (a), the payor must file a copy of the return with the commissioner if:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the return is for a payment made to a Minnesota resident, to a recipient with a Minnesota address, or for activity occurring in the state of Minnesota; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the payment is for wages, salaries, or other compensation for services provided. The commissioner may require this information to be filed in electronic or another form that the commissioner determines is appropriate, notwithstanding the provisions of paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A person, corporation, or cooperative required to file returns under this subdivision must file the returns on magnetic media if magnetic media was used to satisfy the federal reporting requirement under section 6011(e) of the Internal Revenue Code, unless the person establishes to the satisfaction of the commissioner that compliance with this requirement would be an undue hardship.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Returns by brokers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, within 30 days after notice and demand, require a person doing business as a broker to give the commissioner the names and addresses of customers for whom they have transacted business, and the details regarding gross proceeds and other information concerning the transactions as will enable the commissioner to determine whether the income tax due on profits or gains of those customers has been paid. The provisions of section 6045 of the Internal Revenue Code which define terms and require that a statement be furnished to the customer apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Returns by agents.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, within 30 days after notice and demand, require a person acting as agent for another to make a return furnishing the information reasonably necessary to properly assess and collect the tax imposed by chapter 290 upon the person for whom the agent acts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Returns for real property holdings of aliens.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require a person or corporation required to make a return under section 6039C (relating to information return on a foreign person holding a United States real property interest) of the Internal Revenue Code to make a similar return for the commissioner for foreign persons holding a Minnesota real property interest.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Returns for unemployment benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require a person who makes payments of unemployment benefits totaling $10 or more to any individual during a calendar year and who is required to make and file a return under section 6050B of the Internal Revenue Code to file a copy of the return with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Returns for payments of remuneration for services and direct sales.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require a person who is required to make a return under section 6041A (relating to information returns regarding payments of remuneration for services and direct sales) of the Internal Revenue Code to file a copy of the return containing the information required under that section with the commissioner. The provisions of that section govern the requirements of a statement that must be given to persons with respect to whom information is required to be given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Returns relating to Social Security benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require the appropriate federal official who is required to make a return under section 6050F (relating to Social Security benefits) of the Internal Revenue Code to file a copy of the return containing the information required under that section with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Returns by trustees.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require the trustee of an individual retirement account and the issuer of an endowment contract or an individual retirement annuity who is required to make a report under section 408(i) of the Internal Revenue Code to file with the commissioner a copy of that report containing the information required under that subsection. The provisions of that subsection govern when the reports are to be filed and the requirements of a statement that must be given to persons with respect to whom information must be given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Statements to payees.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who can be required to file a return with the commissioner under subdivisions 4 to 10 must furnish to a person whose name is set forth in the return a written statement showing the name and address of the person making the return, and the aggregate amount of payments to the person shown on the return.</p>
          <p>This written statement must be given to the person on or before January 31 of the year following the calendar year for which the return was made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Supplying of Social Security number.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual with respect to whom a return, statement, or other document is required under this section to be made by another person must furnish to that person the individual's Social Security account number. A person required under this section to make a return, statement, or other document with respect to another person who is an individual must request from that individual and must include in the return, statement, or other document the individual's Social Security account number. A return of an estate or trust with respect to its liability for tax, and any statement or other document in its support, is considered a return, statement, or other document with respect to the individual beneficiary of the estate or trust; otherwise, a return of an individual with respect to the individual's liability for tax, or any statement or other document in its support, is not considered a return, statement, or other document with respect to another person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote"> Reporting exempt interest and exempt-interest dividends.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A regulated investment company paying $10 or more in exempt-interest dividends to an individual who is a resident of Minnesota, or any person receiving $10 or more of exempt interest or exempt-interest dividends and paying as nominee to an individual who is a resident of Minnesota, must make a return indicating the amount of the exempt interest or exempt-interest dividends, the name, address, and Social Security number of the recipient, and any other information that the commissioner specifies. The return must be provided to the recipient by February 15 of the year following the year of the payment. The return provided to the recipient must include a clear statement, in the form prescribed by the commissioner, that the exempt interest or exempt-interest dividends must be included in the computation of Minnesota taxable income. By June 1 of each year, the payer must file a copy of the return with the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For purposes of this subdivision, the following definitions apply.</p>
          <p class="in">(1) "Exempt-interest dividends" mean exempt-interest dividends as defined in section 852(b)(5) of the Internal Revenue Code, but does not include the portion of exempt-interest dividends that are not required to be added to federal adjusted gross income under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.0131#stat.290.0131.2">290.0131, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
          <p class="in">(2) "Regulated investment company" means regulated investment company as defined in section 851(a) of the Internal Revenue Code or a fund of the regulated investment company as defined in section 851(g) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p class="in">(3) "Exempt interest" means income on obligations of any state other than Minnesota, or a political or governmental subdivision, municipality, or governmental agency or instrumentality of any state other than Minnesota, and exempt from federal income taxes under the Internal Revenue Code or any other federal statute.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Report of job opportunity zone benefits; penalty for failure to file report.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) By October 15 of each year, every qualified business, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.310#stat.469.310.11">469.310, subdivision 11</a>, must file with the commissioner, on a form prescribed by the commissioner, a report listing the tax benefits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.315">469.315</a> received by the business for the previous year.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall send notice to each business that fails to timely submit the report required under paragraph (a). The notice shall demand that the business submit the report within 60 days. Where good cause exists, the commissioner may extend the period for submitting the report as long as a request for extension is filed by the business before the expiration of the 60-day period. The commissioner shall notify the commissioner of employment and economic development and the appropriate job opportunity subzone administrator whenever notice is sent to a business under this paragraph.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A business that fails to submit the report as required under paragraph (b) is no longer a qualified business under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.310#stat.469.310.11">469.310, subdivision 11</a>, and is subject to the repayment provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.319">469.319</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Qualified intermediaries.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may by notice and demand require a qualified intermediary to file a return relating to transactions for which the intermediary acted to facilitate exchanges under section 1031 of the Internal Revenue Code. The return must include the name, address, and state or federal tax identification number or Social Security number of each of the parties to the exchange, information relating to the property subject to the exchange, and any other information required by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Third-party payers of sick pay benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A third-party payer of sick pay benefits who withholds income tax from the sick pay of an employee as agent for the employer of the employee, and who remits that withholding tax to the commissioner must file an annual report on a form prescribed by the commissioner. The report must include the name and tax identification number of each employer for whom the payer has made sick pay payments and the name, Social Security number, amount of sick pay paid, and amount of tax withheld for each employee.</p>
          <p>(b) The report must be filed with the commissioner on or before February 28 of the year following the year in which the sick pay benefits were paid.</p>
          <p>(c) The report required by this subdivision does not need to be filed if the third-party payer, rather than the employer, has provided to the employee the annual statement required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.09#stat.289A.09.2">289A.09, subdivision 2</a>, that includes the sick pay benefits paid and the tax withheld.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.12.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Returns by qualified heirs.</span></h2>
          <p>A qualified heir, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.03">291.03</a>, subdivision 8, paragraph (c), must file two returns with the commissioner attesting that no disposition or cessation as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.03#stat.291.03.11">291.03, subdivision 11</a>, paragraph (a), occurred. The first return must be filed no earlier than 24 months and no later than 26 months after the decedent's death. The second return must be filed no earlier than 36 months and no later than 39 months after the decedent's death.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.12.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.7.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.6.7.0">1991 c 291 art 6 s 7</a>,<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/292/#laws.6.46.0">46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.6.19.0">1992 c 511 art 6 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.2.9.0">1993 c 375 art 2 s 9</a>-18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.8.14.0">art 8 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/488/#laws.0.8.0">1994 c 488 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.1.24.0">1994 c 587 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/202/#laws.1.25.0">1995 c 202 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/389/#laws.7.12.0">1998 c 389 art 7 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/107/#laws.0.66.0">1999 c 107 s 66</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/343/#laws.0.4.0">2000 c 343 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.7.32.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 7 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.3.1.0">2008 c 154 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.11.4.0">art 11 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.5.8.0">2008 c 366 art 5 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.7.2.0">2009 c 88 art 7 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/389/#laws.3.3.0">2010 c 389 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.1.4.0">2011 c 112 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.15.2.0">2013 c 143 art 15 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/158/#laws.3.4.0">2016 c 158 art 3 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.13.4.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 13 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.1.6.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 1 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.121">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.121 TAX SHELTERS; SPECIAL RULES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to a tax shelter that:</p>
          <p>(1) is organized in this state;</p>
          <p>(2) is doing business in this state;</p>
          <p>(3) is deriving income from sources in this state; or</p>
          <p>(4) has one or more investors that are Minnesota taxpayers under chapter 290.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the definitions under sections 6111, 6112, and 6707A of the Internal Revenue Code, including the regulations under those sections, apply.</p>
          <p>(b) The term "tax shelter" means any reportable transaction as defined under section 6707A(c)(1) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Registration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any material advisor required to register a tax shelter under section 6111 of the Internal Revenue Code must register the shelter with the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) A material advisor subject to this subdivision must send a duplicate of the federal registration information, along with any other information the commissioner requires, to the commissioner not later than the day on which interests in that tax shelter are first offered for sale to Minnesota taxpayers.</p>
          <p>(c) In addition to the requirements under paragraph (b), any listed transaction must be registered with the commissioner by the latest of:</p>
          <p>(1) 60 days after entering into the transaction;</p>
          <p>(2) 60 days after the transaction becomes a listed transaction; or</p>
          <p>(3) October 15, 2005.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Registration number.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person required to register under section 6111 of the Internal Revenue Code who receives a tax registration number from the Secretary of the Treasury must file, within 30 days after requested by the commissioner, a statement of the registration number with the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) Any person who sells or otherwise transfers an interest in a tax shelter must, in the same time and manner required under section 6111(b) of the Internal Revenue Code, furnish to each investor who purchases or otherwise acquires an interest in the tax shelter the identification number assigned under federal law to the tax shelter.</p>
          <p>(c) Any person claiming any deduction, credit, or other tax benefit by reason of a tax shelter must include on the return on which the deduction, credit, or other benefit is claimed the identification number assigned under federal law to the tax shelter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reportable transactions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For each taxable year in which a taxpayer must make a return or a statement under Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, section 1.6011-4, for a reportable transaction, including a listed transaction, in which the taxpayer participated in a taxable year for which a return is required under chapter 290, the taxpayer must file a copy of the disclosure with the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) Any taxpayer that is a member of a unitary business group that includes any person that must make a disclosure statement under Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, section 1.6011-4, must file a disclosure under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(c) Disclosure under this subdivision is required for any transaction entered into after December 31, 2001, that the Internal Revenue Service determines is a listed transaction at any time, and must be made in the manner prescribed by the commissioner. With respect to transactions in which the taxpayer participated for taxable years ending on and after December 31, 2005, disclosure must be made in the time and manner prescribed in Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, section 1.6011-4(e).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.121.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Lists of investors.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person required to maintain a list under section 6112 of the Internal Revenue Code with respect to any reportable transaction must furnish the list to the commissioner no later than when required under federal law. The list required under this subdivision must include the same information required with respect to a reportable transaction under section 6112 of the Internal Revenue Code, and any other information the commissioner requires.</p>
          <p>(b) For transactions entered into on or after December 31, 2001, that become listed transactions at any time, the list must be furnished to the commissioner by the latest of:</p>
          <p>(1) 60 days after entering into the transaction; or</p>
          <p>(2) 60 days after the transaction becomes a listed transaction.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.121.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.8.2.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.8.8.0">2006 c 259 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/40/#laws.1.98.0">2017 c 40 art 1 s 98</a>,99</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.289A.13"><b>289A.13</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.1.117.0">2005 c 151 art 1 s 117</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.14">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.14 USE OF AUTOMATED SALES SUPPRESSION DEVICES; DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <p>(a) For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.60#stat.289A.60.32">289A.60, subdivision 32</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.63#stat.289A.63.12">289A.63, subdivision 12</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.5316#stat.609.5316.3">609.5316, subdivision 3</a>, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
        <p>(b) "Automated sales suppression device" or "zapper" means a software program, carried on any tangible medium, or accessed through any other means, that falsifies the electronic records of electronic cash registers and other point-of-sale systems including, but not limited to, transaction data and transaction reports.</p>
        <p>(c) "Electronic cash register" means a device that keeps a register or supporting documents through the means of an electronic device or computer system designed to record transaction data for the purpose of computing, compiling, or processing retail sales transaction data in whatever manner.</p>
        <p>(d) "Phantom-ware" means hidden preinstalled or later-installed programming option embedded in the operating system of an electronic cash register or hardwired into the electronic cash register that can be used to create a virtual second electronic cash register or may eliminate or manipulate transaction records that may or may not be preserved in digital formats to represent the true or manipulated record of transactions in the electronic cash register.</p>
        <p>(e) "Transaction data" includes items purchased by a customer, the price of each item, the taxability determination for each item, a segregated tax amount for each of the taxed items, the date and time of the purchase, the name, address, and identification number of the vendor, and the receipt or invoice number of the transaction.</p>
        <p>(f) "Transaction report" means a report documenting, but not limited to, the sales, taxes collected, media totals, and discount voids at an electronic cash register that is printed on cash register tape at the end of a day or shift, or a report documenting every action at an electronic cash register that is stored electronically.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.14.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.12.1.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 12 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DUE DATES AND FILING EXTENSIONS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.289A.18">
        <h1 class="shn">289A.18 DUE DATES FOR FILING OF RETURNS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Individual income, fiduciary income, corporate franchise, and entertainment taxes; partnership and S corporation returns; information returns; mining company returns.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The returns required to be made under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.08">289A.08</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.12">289A.12</a> must be filed at the following times:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) returns made on the basis of the calendar year must be filed on April 15 following the close of the calendar year, except that returns of corporations and partnerships must be filed on the due date for filing the federal income tax return;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) returns made on the basis of the fiscal year must be filed on the 15th day of the fourth month following the close of the fiscal year, except that returns of corporations and partnerships must be filed on the due date for filing the federal income tax return;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) returns for a fractional part of a year must be filed on the due date for filing the federal income tax return;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) in the case of a final return of a decedent for a fractional part of a year, the return must be filed on the 15th day of the fourth month following the close of the 12-month period that began with the first day of that fractional part of a year;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) in the case of the return of a cooperative association, returns must be filed on or before the 15th day of the ninth month following the close of the taxable year;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) if a corporation has been divested from a unitary group and files a return for a fractional part of a year in which it was a member of a unitary business that files a combined report under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.17#stat.290.17.4">290.17, subdivision 4</a>, the divested corporation's return must be filed on the 15th day of the third month following the close of the common accounting period that includes the fractional year;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) returns of entertainment entities must be filed on April 15 following the close of the calendar year;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) returns required to be filed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.08#stat.289A.08.4">289A.08, subdivision 4</a>, must be filed on the 15th day of the fifth month following the close of the taxable year;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) returns of mining companies must be filed on May 1 following the close of the calendar year; and</p>
          <p class="in">(10) returns required to be filed with the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.12#stat.289A.12.2">289A.12, subdivision 2</a>, 4 to 10, or 16 must be filed within 30 days after being demanded by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Withholding returns, entertainer withholding returns, returns for withholding from payments to out-of-state contractors, and withholding returns from partnerships and S corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Withholding returns are due on or before the last day of the month following the close of the quarterly period. However, if the return shows timely deposits in full payment of the taxes due for that period, the returns may be filed on or before the tenth day of the second calendar month following the period. An employer, in preparing a quarterly return, may take credit for deposits previously made for that quarter. Entertainer withholding tax returns are due within 30 days after each performance. Returns for withholding from payments to out-of-state contractors are due within 30 days after the payment to the contractor. Returns for withholding by partnerships are due on or before the due date specified for filing partnership returns. Returns for withholding by S corporations are due on or before the due date specified for filing corporate franchise tax returns.</p>
          <p>(b) A seasonal employer who provides notice in the form and manner prescribed by the commissioner before the end of the calendar quarter is not required to file a withholding tax return for periods of anticipated inactivity unless the employer pays wages during the period from which tax is withheld. For purposes of this paragraph, a seasonal employer is an employer that regularly, in the same one or more quarterly periods of each calendar year, pays no wages to employees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Annual withholding returns; eligible employers.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employer who deducts and withholds an amount required to be withheld by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.92">290.92</a> may file an annual return and make an annual payment of the amount required to be deducted and withheld for that calendar year if the employer has received a notification under paragraph (b). The ability to elect to file an annual return continues through the year following the year where an employer is required to deduct and withhold more than $500.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner is authorized to determine which employers are eligible to file an annual return and to notify employers who newly qualify to file an annual return because the amount an employer is required to deduct and withhold for that calendar year is $500 or less based on the most recent period of four consecutive quarters for which the commissioner has compiled data on that employer's withholding tax for that period. At the time of notification, eligible employers may still decide to file returns and make deposits quarterly. An employer who decides to file returns and make deposits quarterly is required to make all returns and deposits required by this chapter and, notwithstanding paragraph (a), is subject to all applicable penalties for failing to do so.</p>
          <p>(c) If, at the end of any calendar month other than the last month of the calendar year, the aggregate amount of undeposited tax withheld by an employer who has elected to file an annual return exceeds $500, the employer must deposit the aggregate amount with the commissioner within 30 days of the end of the calendar month.</p>
          <p>(d) If an employer who has elected to file an annual return ceases to pay wages for which withholding is required, the employer must file a final return and deposit any undeposited tax within 30 days of the end of the calendar month following the month in which the employer ceased paying wages.</p>
          <p>(e) An employer not subject to paragraph (c) or (d) who elects to file an annual return must file the return and pay the tax not previously deposited before February 1 of the year following the year in which the tax was withheld.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Estate tax returns.</span></h2>
          <p>An estate tax return must be filed with the commissioner within nine months after the decedent's death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Recapture tax return.</span></h2>
          <p>A recapture tax return must be filed with the commissioner within six months after the date of the disposition or cessation as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/291.03#stat.291.03.11">291.03, subdivision 11</a>, paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Sales and use tax returns.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Sales and use tax returns must be filed on or before the 20th day of the month following the close of the preceding reporting period, except that annual use tax returns provided for under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.11#stat.289A.11.1">289A.11, subdivision 1</a>, must be filed by April 15 following the close of the calendar year, in the case of individuals. Annual use tax returns of businesses, including sole proprietorships, and annual sales tax returns must be filed by February 5 following the close of the calendar year.</p>
          <p>(b) Returns for the June reporting period filed by retailers required to remit their June liability under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.20#stat.289A.20.4">289A.20, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b), are due on or before August 20.</p>
          <p>(c) If a retailer has an average sales and use tax liability, including local sales and use taxes administered by the commissioner, equal to or less than $500 per month in any quarter of a calendar year, and has substantially complied with the tax laws during the preceding four calendar quarters, the retailer may request authorization to file and pay the taxes quarterly in subsequent calendar quarters. The authorization remains in effect during the period in which the retailer's quarterly returns reflect sales and use tax liabilities of less than $1,500 and there is continued compliance with state tax laws.</p>
          <p>(d) If a retailer has an average sales and use tax liability, including local sales and use taxes administered by the commissioner, equal to or less than $100 per month during a calendar year, and has substantially complied with the tax laws during that period, the retailer may request authorization to file and pay the taxes annually in subsequent years. The authorization remains in effect during the period in which the retailer's annual returns reflect sales and use tax liabilities of less than $1,200 and there is continued compliance with state tax laws.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner may also grant quarterly or annual filing and payment authorizations to retailers if the commissioner concludes that the retailers' future tax liabilities will be less than the monthly totals identified in paragraphs (c) and (d). An authorization granted under this paragraph is subject to the same conditions as an authorization granted under paragraphs (c) and (d).</p>
          <p>(f) A taxpayer who is a materials supplier may report gross receipts either on:</p>
          <p>(1) the cash basis as the consideration is received; or</p>
          <p>(2) the accrual basis as sales are made.</p>
          <p class="bl">As used in this paragraph, "materials supplier" means a person who provides materials for the improvement of real property; who is primarily engaged in the sale of lumber and building materials-related products to owners, contractors, subcontractors, repairers, or consumers; who is authorized to file a mechanics lien upon real property and improvements under chapter 514; and who files with the commissioner an election to file sales and use tax returns on the basis of this paragraph.</p>
          <p>(g) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) to (f), a seller that is not a Model 1, 2, or 3 seller, as those terms are used in the Streamlined Sales and Use Tax Agreement, that does not have a legal requirement to register in Minnesota, and that is registered under the agreement, must file a return by February 5 following the close of the calendar year in which the seller initially registers, and must file subsequent returns on February 5 on an annual basis in succeeding years. Additionally, a return must be submitted on or before the 20th day of the month following any month by which sellers have accumulated state and local tax funds for the state in the amount of $1,000 or more.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.289A.18.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Property tax refund claims.</span></h2>
          <p>A claim for a refund based on property taxes payable must be filed with the commissioner on or before August 15 of the year in which the property taxes are due and payable.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.289A.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.8.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.6.8.0">1991 c 291 art 6 s 8</a>; art 7 s 1; art 8 s 4; art 11 s 4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.8.3.0">1992 c 511 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.2.20.0">1993 c 375 art 2 s 20</a>,21; art 10 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/510/#laws.3.7.0">1994 c 510 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/264/#laws.10.1.0">1995 c 264 art 10 s 1</a>; art 17 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/31/#laws.1.6.0">1997 c 31 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/243/#laws.4.1.0">1999 c 243 art 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/7/#laws.0.56.0">2001 c 7 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.17.8.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 17 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.1.3.0">2003 c 127 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.6.2.0">2005 c 151 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.11.5.0">2008 c 154 art 11 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.12.1.0">2008 c 366 art 12 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.7.3.0">2009 c 88 art 7 s 3</a>;
